\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\hskip-13.45679pt{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{cub}}(t)\vbox to13.00002pt{}{}\,&=\,\frac{\lambda\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(0)}a^{\dagger}_{0}\int\mathrm{d}k\ \Big[u_{t}(0)\Big(\big(1+2\gamma_{t}(k)\big)f_{0}(k)\big(\hat{v}(k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)\phi_{t}\\
&\qquad+2f_{0}(k)\sigma_{t}(k)\hat{v}(k)\overline{\phi}_{t}\Big)\,+\,v_{t}(0)\Big(\big(1+2\gamma_{t}(k)\big)f_{0}(k)\big(\hat{v}(k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)\overline{\phi}_{t}\\
&\qquad+2f_{0}(k)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(k)\hat{v}(k)\phi_{t}\Big)\Big]\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}\,,\end{split}

and

(A.214)

\displaystyle\,=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[\Big(\big(f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma_{t}\big)*\hat{v}(p)(1+\gamma_{t}(p))\,+\,\frac{\big(f_{0}^{(+)}\overline{\sigma}_{t}\big)*\hat{v}(p)\sigma_{t}(p)}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}

(A.215)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\big((1+2\gamma_{t})f_{0}^{(+)}\big)*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)(p)\sigma_{t}(p)\Big)e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

(A.216)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\Big(\big((1+2\gamma_{t})f_{0}^{(+)})*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)(p)(1+2\gamma_{t}(p))

(A.217)

\displaystyle\quad+\,4\operatorname{Re}\big(\big(f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma_{t}\big)*\hat{v}(p)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)\big)\Big)a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\Big]\,.

Proof.

Using symmetry of
\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}(p_{1},p_{2},p_{3})
in
(p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2})
, see Lemma
A.2
, we obtain that

(A.218)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int\mathrm{d}k\ \mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{t}(k,0,k)f_{0}(k)e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(0)}a^{\dagger}_{0}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}\,,

where

(A.219)

\displaystyle\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{t}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\,=

(A.220)

\displaystyle\sqrt{|\Lambda|}\Big(\big(u_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})\phi_{t}+v_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{3})\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2})\big)

(A.221)

\displaystyle+\big(v_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{3})\phi_{t}+u_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{3})\big)

(A.222)

\displaystyle+\big(u_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{3})\phi_{t}+v_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{3})\big)\Big)\,,

Using the facts that
u_{t}=\sqrt{1+\gamma_{t}}
and that
v_{t}=\frac{\sigma_{t}}{\sqrt{1+\gamma_{t}}}
, we thus find that

(A.223)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{\lambda\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\int\mathrm{d}k\ f_{0}(k)e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(0)}a^{\dagger}_{0}

(A.224)

\displaystyle\qquad\Big(\big(u_{t}(0)(1+\gamma_{t}(k))\phi_{t}+v_{t}(0)\gamma_{t}(k)\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)\big(\hat{v}(k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)

(A.225)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,\big(u_{t}(0)\gamma_{t}(k)\phi_{t}+v_{t}(0)(1+\gamma_{t}(k))\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)\big(\hat{v}(k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)

(A.226)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,\big(v_{t}(0)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(k)\phi_{t}+u_{t}(0)\sigma_{t}(k)\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)2\hat{v}(k)\Big)\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

(A.227)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{\lambda\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\int\mathrm{d}k\ f_{0}(k)e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(0)}a^{\dagger}_{0}

(A.228)

\displaystyle\qquad\Big(\big(\hat{v}(k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)\big(1+2\gamma_{t}(k)\big)\big(u_{t}(0)\phi_{t}+v_{t}(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)

(A.229)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,2\hat{v}(k)\big(v_{t}(0)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(k)\phi_{t}+u_{t}(0)\sigma_{t}(k)\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)\Big)\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}\,,

where we also used the evenness of
\sigma_{t}
and
\gamma_{t}
. Sorting the terms by the coefficients
u_{t}(0)
and
v_{t}(0)
, we obtain

(A.230)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{\lambda\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(0)}a^{\dagger}_{0}\int\mathrm{d}k\ \Big[u_{t}(0)\Big(\big(1+2\gamma_{t}(k)\big)f_{0}(k)\big(\hat{v}(k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)\phi_{t}

(A.231)

\displaystyle\qquad+2f_{0}(k)\sigma_{t}(k)\hat{v}(k)\overline{\phi}_{t}\Big)\,+\,v_{t}(0)\Big(\big(1+2\gamma_{t}(k)\big)f_{0}(k)\big(\hat{v}(k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)\overline{\phi}_{t}

(A.232)

\displaystyle\qquad+2f_{0}(k)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(k)\hat{v}(k)\phi_{t}\Big)\Big]\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}\,.

Similarly, the symmetries of
\mathbf{w}^{(3,1)}_{t}(\mathbf{p}_{4})
in
(p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}\leftrightarrow p_{3})
, and of
\mathbf{w}^{(2,2)}_{t}(\mathbf{p}_{4})
in
(p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2})
and
(p_{3}\leftrightarrow p_{4})
, see Lemma
A.2
, imply

(A.233)

\displaystyle\,=\,

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \mathrm{d}k\ f_{0}(k)\Big(\frac{\mathbf{w}^{(3,1)}_{t}(p,-p,k,k)}{2}e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

(A.234)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\mathbf{w}^{(2,2)}_{t}(p,k,k,p)a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\Big)\,,

where

(A.235)

\displaystyle\mathbf{w}^{(3,1)}_{t}(\mathbf{p}_{4})\,:=

(A.236)

\displaystyle\big(u_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})v_{t}(p_{3})u_{t}(p_{4})+v_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{4})\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{1}+p_{3})+\hat{v}(p_{2}+p_{3})\big)

(A.237)

\displaystyle+\big(u_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})u_{t}(p_{4})+v_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})v_{t}(p_{3})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{4})\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{1}+p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{2}+p_{3})\big)

(A.238)

\displaystyle+\,\big(v_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})u_{t}(p_{4})+u_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})v_{t}(p_{3})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{4})\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{1}+p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{1}+p_{3})\big)\,,

(A.239)

\displaystyle\mathbf{w}^{(2,2)}_{t}(\mathbf{p}_{4})\,:=

(A.240)

\displaystyle\big(u_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})u_{t}(p_{4})\,+\,v_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{3})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{3})\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{1}-p_{3})+\hat{v}(p_{2}-p_{3})\big)

(A.241)

\displaystyle+\,\big(u_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{3})u_{t}(p_{4})+v_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{4})\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{1}+p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{2}-p_{3})\big)

(A.242)

\displaystyle+\,\big(v_{t}(p_{1})u_{t}(p_{2})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{3})u_{t}(p_{4})+u_{t}(p_{1})v_{t}(p_{2})u_{t}(p_{3})\overline{v_{t}}(p_{4})\big)\big(\hat{v}(p_{1}+p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{1}-p_{3})\big)\,.

Again using the relations between
u_{t}
,
v_{t}
and
\gamma_{t}
,
\sigma_{t}
, we obtain

(A.243)

\displaystyle\,=

(A.244)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \mathrm{d}k\ f_{0}(k)

(A.245)

\displaystyle\quad\Big[\Big(\big((1+\gamma_{t}(p))\sigma_{t}(k)\,+\,\frac{\sigma_{t}(p)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(k)}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}\big)2\hat{v}(p-k)+\,2\big(\sigma_{t}(p)(1+\gamma_{t}(k))

(A.246)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sigma_{t}(p)\gamma_{t}(k)\big)\big(\hat{v}(p-k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)\Big)\frac{1}{2}e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

(A.247)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\Big(\big((1+\gamma_{t}(p))(1+\gamma_{t}(k))\,+\,\gamma_{t}(p)\gamma_{t}(k)\big)\big(\hat{v}(p-k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)

(A.248)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,\big((1+\gamma_{t}(p))\gamma_{t}(k)\,+\,\gamma_{t}(p)(1+\gamma_{t}(k))\big)\big(\hat{v}(p-k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)

(A.249)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,(\sigma_{t}(p)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(k)\,+\,\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)\sigma_{t}(k))2\hat{v}(p-k)\Big)a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\Big]

(A.250)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \mathrm{d}k\ f_{0}(k)\Big[\Big(\big((1+\gamma_{t}(p))\sigma_{t}(k)\,+\,\frac{\sigma_{t}(p)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(k)}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}\big)\hat{v}(p-k)

(A.251)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,\big(\sigma_{t}(p)(1+\gamma_{t}(k))\,+\,\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)\gamma_{t}(k)\big)\hat{v}(p-k)\Big)e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

(A.252)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\Big((1+2\gamma_{t}(p))(1+2\gamma_{t}(k))\big(\hat{v}(p-k)+\hat{v}(0)\big)

(A.253)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,4\operatorname{Re}\big(\sigma_{t}(p)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(k)\big)\hat{v}(p-k)\Big)a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\Big]\,.

Abbreviating
f_{0}^{(+)}=\frac{1}{2}(f_{0}(p)+f_{0}(-p))
, see (
2.38
) and using evenness of
\sigma
,
\gamma
,
\hat{v}
, we can simplify the expression as

(A.254)

\displaystyle\,=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[\Big(\big(f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma_{t}\big)*\hat{v}(p)(1+\gamma_{t}(p))\,+\,\frac{\big(f_{0}^{(+)}\overline{\sigma}_{t}\big)*\hat{v}(p)\sigma_{t}(p)}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}

(A.255)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\big((1+2\gamma_{t})f_{0}^{(+)}\big)*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)(p)\sigma_{t}(p)\Big)e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

(A.256)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\Big(\big((1+2\gamma_{t})f_{0}^{(+)})*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)(p)(1+2\gamma_{t}(p))

(A.257)

\displaystyle\quad+\,4\operatorname{Re}\big(\big(f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma_{t}\big)*\hat{v}(p)\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)\big)\Big)a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\Big]\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma A.4

(HFB equations)
.

Let

(A.258)

\displaystyle\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}(p):=

\displaystyle\gamma_{t}(p)\,+\,N|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\delta(p)\,,

(A.259)

\displaystyle\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}(p):=

\displaystyle\sigma_{t}(p)\,+\,N|\Lambda|\phi_{t}^{2}\delta(p)\,.

Then

(A.260)

\displaystyle\frac{i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}(p)}{2}\,-\,\frac{\sigma_{t}(p)i\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}(p)}{2(1+\gamma_{t}(p))}\,=\,

(A.261)

\displaystyle\Big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)(p)\Big)\sigma_{t}(p)

(A.262)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda}{2N}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(p)(1+\gamma_{t}(p))\,+\,\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(p)\frac{\sigma_{t}(p)^{2}}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}\Big)

is equivalent to

(A.263)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\big[\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\,\overline{\sigma}_{t}\,-\,\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\,\sigma_{t}\big]\,,

(A.264)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}=

\displaystyle 2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\sigma_{t}\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\gamma_{t})\,.

Proof.

Multiplying (
A.262
) by
\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)
, and taking imaginary parts, the l.h.s. of (
A.262
) reads

(A.265)

\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{Re}(\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}(p))}{2}\,-\,\frac{|\sigma_{t}(p)|^{2}\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}(p)}{2(1+\gamma_{t}(p))}

(A.266)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\partial_{t}\big(|\sigma_{t}(p)|^{2}\,-\,\gamma_{t}(p)^{2}\big)

(A.267)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}(p)\,,

where we employed (
2.32
). Thus, we have that

(A.268)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{2N}\operatorname{Im}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+\gamma_{t})\overline{\sigma}_{t}\,+\,\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\frac{|\sigma_{t}|^{2}\sigma_{t}}{1+\gamma_{t}}\Big)\,,

which, using (
2.32
), is equivalent to

(A.269)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}(p)=

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+\gamma_{t})\overline{\sigma}_{t}\,+\,\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\gamma_{t}\sigma_{t}\Big)

(A.270)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\overline{\sigma}_{t}\Big)\,.

Substituting this identity into (
A.262
), we find that

(A.271)

\displaystyle\frac{i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}(p)}{2}\,-\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\frac{\sigma_{t}(p)}{2(1+\gamma_{t}(p))}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\overline{\sigma}_{t}\,-\,\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\sigma_{t}\Big)\,=\,

(A.272)

\displaystyle\Big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)(p)\Big)\sigma_{t}(p)

(A.273)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda}{2N}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(p)(1+\gamma_{t}(p))\,+\,\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(p)\frac{\sigma_{t}(p)^{2}}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}\Big)\,.

Isolating
i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}
on the l.h.s. and using (
2.32
), we obtain that

(A.274)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}=

\displaystyle 2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\sigma_{t}\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\gamma_{t})\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma A.5

(Bogoliubov dispersion)
.

We have that
{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(d)}}(t)=0
if

(A.275)

\displaystyle\Omega_{t}(p)\,=\,

(A.276)

\displaystyle\Big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\big((\gamma_{t}+N|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\delta)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)\Big)\big(1+2\gamma_{t}(p)\big)

(A.277)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big((\overline{\sigma}_{t}+N|\Lambda|\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}\delta)*\hat{v}\big)(p)\sigma_{t}(p)\Big)\,-\frac{\operatorname{Re}\big(\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}(p)\big)}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}\,.

If
\sigma_{t}
satisfies (
A.264
), we have that

(A.278)

\displaystyle\Omega^{(1)}_{t}\,=\,E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\frac{\operatorname{Re}\big((\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v})\overline{\sigma}^{(1)}_{t}\big)}{1+\gamma^{(1)}_{t}}\,.

Proof.

Lemma
A.2
implies that
{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(d)}}(t)=0
if

(A.279)

\displaystyle\Omega_{t}(p)\,=\,

(A.280)

\displaystyle\Big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\big((\gamma_{t}+N|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\delta)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)\Big)\big(1+2\gamma_{t}(p)\big)

(A.281)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big((\overline{\sigma}_{t}+N|\Lambda|\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}\delta)*\hat{v}\big)(p)\sigma_{t}(p)\Big)\,-\frac{\operatorname{Re}\big(\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}(p)\big)}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}\,.

Substituting (
3.38
) in this expression and recalling definitions (
3.30
) of
\Gamma^{(1)}
and (
3.31
) of
\Sigma^{(1)}
, we obtain

(A.282)

\displaystyle\Omega^{(1)}_{t}=

\displaystyle-2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\frac{|\sigma^{(1)}_{t}|^{2}}{1+\gamma^{(1)}_{t}}+\big[E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)\big](1+2\gamma^{(1)}_{t})

(A.283)

\displaystyle-\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\overline{\sigma}^{(1)}_{t}\Big)\frac{1+2\gamma_{t}}{1+\gamma^{(1)}_{t}}\,+\,\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\overline{\sigma}^{(1)}_{t}\Big)\,.

Recalling that
|\sigma_{t}|^{2}=\gamma_{t}(1+\gamma_{t})
, see (
2.32
), this results in

(A.284)

\displaystyle\Omega^{(1)}_{t}\,=\,E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(1)}_{t}*\big(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)\big)\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\frac{\operatorname{Re}\big((\Sigma^{(1)}_{t}*\hat{v})\overline{\sigma}^{(1)}_{t}\big)}{1+\gamma^{(1)}_{t}}\,.

This finishes the proof.
∎

Appendix B
HFB system analysis

Lemma B.1

(Generalized HFB equations)
.

Let
(\phi,\gamma,\sigma)
satisfy either of the renormalized equations

(B.1)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\phi_{t}=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\Big(\big(\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\phi_{t}\,+\,(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}\Big)

(B.2)

\displaystyle-\,2\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)|\phi_{t}|^{2}\phi_{t}\,,

(B.3)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\big[\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\,\overline{\sigma}_{t}\,-\,\big(\overline{\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}}*\hat{v}\big)\,\sigma_{t}\big]\,,

(B.4)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}=

\displaystyle 2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\sigma_{t}\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\gamma_{t})

for
j=0,1
, where

(B.5)

\displaystyle\Gamma^{(j)}=

\displaystyle(1+2\delta_{1,j}f_{0}^{(+)})\gamma\,+\,\delta_{1,j}f_{0}^{(+)}\,+\,N|\Lambda||\phi|^{2}\delta\,,

(B.6)

\displaystyle\Sigma^{(j)}=

\displaystyle(1+2\delta_{1,j}f_{0}^{(+)})\sigma\,+\,N|\Lambda|\phi^{2}\delta\,,

see definitions (
3.30
), (
3.31
), (
3.56
) and (
3.57
). Then
(\phi,\Gamma^{(j)},\Sigma^{(j)})
satisfies

(B.7)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\phi_{t}=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\Big(\big(\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\phi_{t}\,+\,(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}\Big)

(B.8)

\displaystyle-\,2\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)|\phi_{t}|^{2}\phi_{t}\,,

(B.9)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\Big(\big(\overline{\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}}*\hat{v}\big)\,\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}\Big)\,,

(B.10)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}=

\displaystyle 2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\Gamma^{(j)}_{t})

(B.11)

\displaystyle-\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{2}\hat{v}(0)|\phi|^{2}\phi^{2}\delta

for
j=0,1
.

Proof.

We start with

(B.12)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}|\phi_{t}|^{2}=

\displaystyle 2\operatorname{Re}(\overline{\phi}_{t}\partial_{t}\phi_{t})

(B.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\operatorname{Im}(\overline{\phi}_{t}i\partial_{t}\phi_{t})

(B.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\Big((\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}\Big)\,.

As a consequence, (
B.3
) implies that

(B.15)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}=

\displaystyle(1+2\delta_{1,j}f_{0}^{(+)})\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}\,+\,N|\Lambda|\partial_{t}|\phi_{t}|^{2}\delta

(B.16)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\Big(\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)\,\big((1+2\delta_{1,j}f_{0}^{(+)})\overline{\sigma}_{t}+N|\Lambda|\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}\delta\big)\Big)

(B.17)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\Big(\big(\overline{\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}}*\hat{v}\big)\,\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}\Big)\,.

Similarly, we obtain, using (
B.2
) and (
B.4
), that

(B.18)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}=

\displaystyle(1+2\delta_{1,j}f_{0}^{(+)})i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}\,+\,2N|\Lambda|\phi_{t}i\partial_{t}\phi_{t}\delta

(B.19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+2\delta_{1,j}f_{0}^{(+)})\Big(2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\sigma_{t}

(B.20)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\gamma_{t})\Big)\,+\,2\lambda|\Lambda|\phi_{t}\delta\Big(\big(\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\phi_{t}

(B.21)

\displaystyle+\,(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v})\overline{\phi}_{t}\Big)\,-\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{2}\hat{v}(0)|\phi_{t}|^{2}\phi_{t}^{2}

(B.22)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(j)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\Gamma^{(j)}_{t})

(B.23)

\displaystyle-\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{2}\hat{v}(0)|\phi|^{2}\phi^{2}\delta\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma B.2
.

Assume that
\hat{v}\in L^{1}_{\sqrt{1+E}}\cap L^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})
. Recall the definition of the nonlinearity
\vec{{\mathcal{J}}}=({\mathcal{J}}_{1},{\mathcal{J}}_{2},{\mathcal{J}}_{3})
in the HFB equations, where

(B.24)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{1}(\phi,\Gamma,\Sigma)=

\displaystyle-\,i\Big[\frac{\lambda}{N}\Big(\big(\Gamma*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\phi\,+\,(\Sigma*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}\Big)\,,

(B.25)

\displaystyle-\,2\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)|\phi|^{2}\phi\Big]

(B.26)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{2}(\phi,\Gamma,\Sigma)=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\Big(\big(\overline{\Sigma}*\hat{v}\big)\,\Sigma\Big)\,,

(B.27)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi,\Gamma,\Sigma)=

\displaystyle-\,i\Big[2\big(\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\Sigma\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\Gamma)

(B.28)

\displaystyle-\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{2}\hat{v}(0)|\phi|^{2}\phi^{2}\,\delta\Big]\,,

see (
4.3
)–(
4.7
). Then there is a constant
C>0
such that we have that

(B.29)

\displaystyle\|\vec{{\mathcal{J}}}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,\vec{{\mathcal{J}}}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{{\mathcal{X}}^{1}}

(B.30)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\frac{\lambda}{N}(\|\hat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\sqrt{1+E}}}+\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty})\big(\|(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\|_{{\mathcal{X}}^{1}}+\|(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{{\mathcal{X}}^{1}}\big)

(B.31)

\displaystyle\|(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2},\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})\|_{{\mathcal{X}}^{1}}\,+\,\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)(|\phi_{1}|^{2}+|\phi_{2}|^{2})

(B.32)

\displaystyle\big(1+N|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}(|\phi_{1}|+|\phi_{2}|)\big)|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|

Proof.

{\mathcal{J}}_{1}
satisfies

(B.33)

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{J}}_{1}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{1}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})|

(B.34)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\Big(|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}|*(|\hat{v}|+|\hat{v}(0)|)(0)|\phi_{1}|\,+\,|\Gamma_{2}|*(|\hat{v}|+|\hat{v}(0)|)(0)|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|

(B.35)

\displaystyle+\,\big(|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}|*|\hat{v}|\big)(0)|\phi_{1}|\,+\,\big(|\Sigma_{2}|*|\hat{v}|\big)(0)|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|\Big)

(B.36)

\displaystyle+\,2\lambda|\Lambda||\hat{v}(0)|\big||\phi_{1}|^{2}\phi_{1}-|\phi_{2}|^{2}\phi_{2}\big|\,.

Using the fact that

(B.37)

\displaystyle|\phi_{1}|^{2}\,-\,|\phi_{2}|^{2}\,=\,\operatorname{Re}\big[(\overline{\phi}_{1}-\overline{\phi}_{2})(\phi_{1}+\phi_{2})\big]\,,

we thus obtain that

(B.38)

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{J}}_{1}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{1}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})|

(B.39)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\Big(2\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty}\big(|\phi_{1}|\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{1}\,+\,\|\Gamma_{2}\|_{1}|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|\big)

(B.40)

\displaystyle+\,\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty}\big(\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{1}|\phi_{1}|\,+\,\|\Sigma_{2}\|_{1}|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|\big)\Big)

(B.41)

\displaystyle+\,4\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)(|\phi_{1}|^{2}+|\phi_{2}|^{2})|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|\,.

Next, we find that

(B.42)

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{J}}_{2}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{2}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{L^{1}_{1+E}}

(B.43)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \big|(\overline{\Sigma}_{1}-\overline{\Sigma}_{2})*\hat{v}(p)\,\Sigma_{1}(p)\,+\,\overline{\Sigma}_{2}*\hat{v}(p)\,(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})(p)\,\big|\big(1+E(p)\big)

(B.44)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\|(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})*\hat{v}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\big(\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}+\|\Sigma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\big)

by Cauchy-Schwarz, where we also used that
\int\mathrm{d}p\ f*g(p)h(p)=\int\mathrm{d}p\ h*g(p)f(p)
. In addition, observe that Young’s inequality implies

(B.45)

\displaystyle\|(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})*\hat{v}\|_{2}\,\leq\,\|\hat{v}\|_{1}\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{2}\,.

Using
E(p)=\frac{1}{2}|p|^{2}
and

(B.46)

\displaystyle\sqrt{E(p)}\,\leq\,\sqrt{E(p-q)}+\sqrt{E(q)}\,,

Young’s inequality yields

(B.47)

\displaystyle\|\big((\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})*\hat{v}\big)\sqrt{E}\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\big(\sqrt{E}(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})\big)*\hat{v}\|_{2}\,+\,\|(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})*(\sqrt{E}\hat{v})\|_{2}

(B.48)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\sqrt{E}(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})\|_{2}\|\hat{v}\|_{1}\,+\,\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{2}\|\sqrt{E}\hat{v}\|_{1}\,.

Collecting (
B.44
), (
B.45
), and (
B.48
), we conclude that

(B.49)

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{J}}_{2}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{2}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{L^{1}_{1+E}}

(B.50)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\big(\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}+\|\Sigma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\big)\|\hat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\sqrt{1+E}}}

(B.51)

\displaystyle(\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}+\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{\infty})\,.

With similar steps, we arrive at

(B.52)

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{J}}_{2}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{2}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{\infty}

(B.53)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\|(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})*\hat{v}\|_{\infty}\big(\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{\infty}+\|\Sigma_{2}\|_{\infty}\big)

(B.54)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\|\hat{v}\|_{1}\big(\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{\infty}+\|\Sigma_{2}\|_{\infty}\big)\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{\infty}\,.

Finally,
{\mathcal{J}}_{3}
satisfies

(B.55)

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.56)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\Big(\|(\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2})*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\,+\,\|\Gamma_{2}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.57)

\displaystyle+\,\|(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})*\hat{v}\Gamma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\,+\,\|\Sigma_{2}*\hat{v}(\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\|(\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2})*\hat{v}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.58)

\displaystyle+\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{5}{2}}\hat{v}(0)\big||\phi_{1}|^{2}\phi_{1}^{2}-|\phi_{2}|^{2}\phi_{2}^{2}\big|\,.

Again, Cauchy-Schwarz implies

(B.59)

\displaystyle\|(\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2})*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.60)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2})*\hat{v}\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\,+\,\hat{v}(0)\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{1}\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.61)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\big(\|(\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2})*\hat{v}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\,+\,\hat{v}(0)\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{1}\big)\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\,.

(
B.45
) and (
B.48
) hence yield

(B.62)

\displaystyle\|(\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2})*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.63)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\big(\|\hat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\sqrt{1+E}}}\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\,+\,\hat{v}(0)\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{1}\big)\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\,.

Moreover, by (
B.37
), we have that

(B.64)

\displaystyle\big||\phi_{1}|^{2}\phi_{1}^{2}-|\phi_{2}|^{2}\phi_{2}^{2}\big|\leq

\displaystyle|\phi_{1}|^{2}|\phi_{1}^{2}-\phi_{2}^{2}|+|\phi_{2}^{2}\big||\phi_{1}|^{2}-|\phi_{2}|^{2}\big|

(B.65)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(|\phi_{1}|^{2}+|\phi_{2}|^{2})(|\phi_{1}|+|\phi_{2}|)|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|\,.

Then (
B.58
), (
B.63
), (
B.65
) and analogous estimates to those for
{\mathcal{J}}_{2}
imply that

(B.66)

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.67)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}(\|\hat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\sqrt{1+E}}}+\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty})\big((\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}+\|\Sigma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}})\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.68)

\displaystyle+\,(\|\Gamma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}+\|\Gamma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}+\frac{1}{2})\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\big)

(B.69)

\displaystyle+\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{5}{2}}\hat{v}(0)(|\phi_{1}|^{2}+|\phi_{2}|^{2})(|\phi_{1}|+|\phi_{2}|)|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|\,.

Observe that

(B.70)

\displaystyle\|\Gamma\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\Gamma\|_{L^{1}_{1+E}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Gamma\|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}

(B.71)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{2}(\|\Gamma\|_{L^{1}_{1+E}}+\|\Gamma\|_{\infty})\,,

which is why

(B.72)

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}

(B.73)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}(\|\hat{v}\|_{L^{1}_{\sqrt{1+E}}}+\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty})\big((\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}+\|\Sigma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}})(\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{L^{1}_{1+E}}+\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{\infty})

(B.74)

\displaystyle+\,(\|\Gamma_{1}\|_{L^{1}_{1+E}}+\|\Gamma_{1}\|_{\infty}+\|\Gamma_{2}\|_{L^{1}_{1+E}}+\|\Gamma_{2}\|_{\infty}+1)\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{1+E}}\big)

(B.75)

\displaystyle+\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{5}{2}}\hat{v}(0)(|\phi_{1}|^{2}+|\phi_{2}|^{2})(|\phi_{1}|+|\phi_{2}|)|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|\,.

Similarly, we obtain that

(B.76)

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi_{1},\Gamma_{1},\Sigma_{1})\,-\,{\mathcal{J}}_{3}(\phi_{2},\Gamma_{2},\Sigma_{2})\|_{\infty}

(B.77)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\lambda}{N}\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty}\big((\|\Sigma_{1}\|_{\infty}+\|\Sigma_{2}\|_{\infty})\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{2}\|_{1}

(B.78)

\displaystyle+\,(\|\Gamma_{1}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma_{2}\|_{1}+1)\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{\infty}\big)

(B.79)

\displaystyle+\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{5}{2}}\hat{v}(0)(|\phi_{1}|^{2}+|\phi_{2}|^{2})(|\phi_{1}|+|\phi_{2}|)|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}|\,.

∎

Proof of Lemma
4.2
.

As a consequence of
(\phi,\Gamma,\Sigma)
being a mild solution, we have that

(B.80)

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Gamma_{t}(p)=

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Gamma_{0}(p)\,-\,\frac{2\lambda}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \operatorname{Im}\langle\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v},\Sigma_{s}\rangle\,.

Let
\widecheck{f}
denote the inverse Fourier transform of
f
. Observe that due to Plancherel,

(B.81)

\displaystyle\langle\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v},\Sigma_{s}\rangle\,=\,\int dx\,|\widecheck{\Sigma}_{s}(x)|^{2}v(x)

is a real number, as
\|v\|_{\infty}\leq\|\hat{v}\|_{1}<\infty
,
v
is real-valued, and
\Sigma_{s}\in\ell^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})
. As a consequence, (
B.80
) implies

(B.82)

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Gamma_{t}(p)\,=\,\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Gamma_{0}(p)\,,

as desired.

In order to show energy-conservation, we split the energy into its individual terms. From equation (
3.77
), we have that

(B.83)

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}p\ E(p)\Gamma_{t}(p)\,=\,\int\mathrm{d}p\ E(p)\Gamma_{0}(p)-\frac{2\lambda}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \langle\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v},E\Sigma_{s}\rangle\,,

where we notice that
\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v}\in L^{2}_{1+E}\cap L^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})
. In particular, all terms in (
B.83
) are finite. Next, we have that

(B.84)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{2N}\langle\Gamma_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)),\Gamma_{t}\rangle-\frac{\lambda}{2N}\langle\Gamma_{0}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)),\Gamma_{0}\rangle

(B.85)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \langle\Gamma_{s}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)),\partial_{s}\Gamma_{s}\rangle

(B.86)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{N^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \langle\Gamma_{s}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0)),\operatorname{Im}\Big((\overline{\Sigma}_{s}*\hat{v})\Sigma_{s}\Big)\rangle

(B.87)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{N^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \operatorname{Im}\langle\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v},(\Gamma_{s}*\hat{v})\Sigma_{s}\rangle\,,

where the expressions in each line are finite. Using the fact that
\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v}\in L^{2}_{1+E}\cap L^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})
, equation (
3.77
) for
\Sigma

(B.88)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{2N}\langle\Sigma_{t}*\hat{v},\Sigma_{t}\rangle\,-\,\frac{\lambda}{2N}\langle\Sigma_{0}*\hat{v},\Sigma_{0}\rangle

(B.89)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \operatorname{Im}\langle\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v},i\partial_{s}\Sigma_{s}\rangle

(B.90)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \int\mathrm{d}p\ (\overline{\Sigma}_{s}*\hat{v})(p)\Big(2\big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma_{s}*(\hat{v}(p)+\hat{v}(0))\big)\Sigma_{s}(p))

(B.91)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v}(p)\big)(1+2\Gamma_{s}(p))\,-\,4N\lambda|\Lambda|^{2}\hat{v}(0)|\phi_{s}|^{2}\phi_{s}^{2}\delta(p)\Big)

(B.92)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \Big(\langle\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v},(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma_{s}*\hat{v})\Sigma_{s}\rangle

(B.93)

\displaystyle-\,2N\lambda|\Lambda|^{2}\hat{v}(0)|\phi_{s}|^{2}(\overline{\Sigma}_{s}*\hat{v})(0)\phi_{s}^{2}\Big)\,.

In addition, equation (
3.77
) for
\phi
implies

(B.94)

\displaystyle-N|\Lambda|^{2}\lambda\hat{v}(0)\big(|\phi_{t}|^{4}-|\phi_{0}|^{4}\big)

(B.95)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2N|\Lambda|^{2}\lambda\hat{v}(0)\operatorname{Im}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ |\phi_{s}|^{2}\overline{\phi}_{s}i\partial_{s}\phi_{s}

(B.96)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2|\Lambda|^{2}\lambda^{2}\hat{v}(0)\operatorname{Im}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ |\phi_{s}|^{2}(\Sigma_{s}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{s}^{2}

(B.97)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2|\Lambda|^{2}\lambda^{2}\hat{v}(0)\operatorname{Im}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ |\phi_{s}|^{2}(\overline{\Sigma}_{s}*\hat{v})(0)\phi_{s}^{2}\,.

Collecting (
B.83
), (
B.87
), (
B.93
), (
B.97
), we obtain that
{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}(\phi_{t},\Gamma_{t},\Sigma_{t})
is differentiable w.r.t. t, and that

(B.98)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}(\phi_{t},\Gamma_{t},\Sigma_{t})\,=\,0\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Appendix C
Boltzmann evolution

Lemma C.1

(Calculation of collision operators)
.

Using the notation in Lemma
A.2
, the cubic Boltzmann operator is given by

(C.1)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ Q_{3}[f_{0}](s,p)\,=\,

(C.2)

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{N}\operatorname{Re}\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{s}_{2}\ \mathds{1}_{s_{1}\geq s_{2}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{3}\ \Big(\frac{1}{2!}\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)-\delta(p_{3}-p)\big)

(C.3)

\displaystyle\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})\big)}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})

(C.4)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\big)

(C.5)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{3!}\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)+\delta(p_{3}-p)\big)

(C.6)

\displaystyle\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(3,0)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})\big)}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})

(C.7)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)\Big)\,,

and the quartic Boltzmann operator is given by

(C.8)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ Q_{4}[f_{0}](s,p)\,=\,

(C.9)

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{N^{2}}\operatorname{Re}\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{s}_{2}\ \mathds{1}_{s_{1}\geq s_{2}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{4}\ \Big(\frac{1}{(2!)^{2}}\mathbf{w}^{(2,2)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,2)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})

(C.10)

\displaystyle\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)-\delta(p_{3}-p)-\delta(p_{4}-p)\big)

(C.11)

\displaystyle\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3}-p_{4})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{4})\big)}

(C.12)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})f_{0}(p_{4})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{4})\big)

(C.13)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{3!}\mathbf{w}^{(3,1)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(3,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})

(C.14)

\displaystyle\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)+\delta(p_{3}-p)-\delta(p_{4}-p)\big)

(C.15)

\displaystyle\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}-p_{4})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{4})\big)}

(C.16)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})f_{0}(p_{4})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{4})\big)

(C.17)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4!}\mathbf{w}^{(4,0)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(4,0)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})

(C.18)

\displaystyle\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)+\delta(p_{3}-p)+\delta(p_{4}-p)\big)

(C.19)

\displaystyle\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}+p_{4})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{4})\big)}

(C.20)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{4})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})f_{0}(p_{4})\big)\Big)\,.

Proof.

Recall that Lemma
A.2
implies

(C.21)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{cub}}(t)\,:=

(C.22)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{3}\ \Big(\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})\big)}

(C.23)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3!}\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{t}(\mathbf{p}_{3})a^{\dagger}_{p_{1}}a^{\dagger}_{p_{2}}a^{\dagger}_{p_{3}}

(C.24)

\displaystyle+\,\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})\big)}

(C.25)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2!}\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{t}(\mathbf{p}_{3})a^{\dagger}_{p_{1}}a^{\dagger}_{p_{2}}a_{p_{3}}

(C.26)

\displaystyle+\,h.c.\Big)\,.

Using (
3.41
), we hence have that

(C.27)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ Q_{3}[f_{0}](s,p)\,=\,

(C.28)

(C.29)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\langle[[\hbox to9.45pt{\vbox to8.99pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.72627pt\lower-4.49501pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin}

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-4.72627pt}{-4.49501pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}

(C.35)

\displaystyle e^{i\int_{0}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})\big)-i\int_{0}^{s_{2}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(k_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(k_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(k_{3})\big)}

(C.36)

\displaystyle+\,\mbox{all\ contractions\ of\ the\ form \eqref{eq-cub-bol-con-1}--\eqref{eq-cub-bol-con-2}}\Big]\,.

\displaystyle+\,\mbox{all\ contractions\ of\ the\ form \eqref{eq-cub-bol-con-1}--\eqref{eq-cub-bol-con-2}}

(C.42)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)-\delta(p_{3}-p)\big)

(C.43)

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{\pi\in{\mathcal{S}}_{2}}\delta(p_{1}-k_{\pi(1)})\delta(p_{2}-k_{\pi(2)})\Big)\delta(p_{3}-k_{3})

(C.44)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)\,,

and similarly

\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}_{k_{1}}]\vbox to22.00002pt{}{}\rangle_{0}

(C.46)

\displaystyle+\,\mbox{all\ contractions\ of\ the\ form \eqref{eq-cub-bol-con-1}--\eqref{eq-cub-bol-con-2}}

(C.47)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)+\delta(p_{3}-p)\big)

(C.48)

\displaystyle\sum_{\pi\in{\mathcal{S}}_{3}}\delta(p_{1}-k_{\pi(1)})\delta(p_{2}-k_{\pi(2)})\delta(p_{3}-k_{\pi(3)})

(C.49)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\big)\,.

Exploiting the symmetry of
\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{s}(\mathbf{k}_{3})
w.r.t. permutations of
(k_{1},k_{2})
and the symmetry of
\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{s}(\mathbf{k}_{3})
w.r.t. permutations of
(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})
, (
C.36
) yields

(C.50)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ Q_{3}[f_{0}](s,p)\,=\,

(C.51)

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{N}\operatorname{Re}\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{s}_{2}\ \mathds{1}_{s_{1}\geq s_{2}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{3}\ \Big(\frac{1}{2!}\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)-\delta(p_{3}-p)\big)

(C.52)

\displaystyle\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})\big)}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})

(C.53)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\big)

(C.54)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{3!}\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)+\delta(p_{3}-p)\big)

(C.55)

\displaystyle\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(3,0)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})\big)}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})

(C.56)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)\Big)\,.

With analogous steps, one obtains

(C.57)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ Q_{4}[f_{0}](s,p)\,=\,

(C.58)

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{N^{2}}\operatorname{Re}\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{s}_{2}\ \mathds{1}_{s_{1}\geq s_{2}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{4}\ \Big(\frac{1}{(2!)^{2}}\mathbf{w}^{(2,2)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,2)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})

(C.59)

\displaystyle\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)-\delta(p_{3}-p)-\delta(p_{4}-p)\big)

(C.60)

\displaystyle\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3}-p_{4})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{4})\big)}

(C.61)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})f_{0}(p_{4})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{4})\big)

(C.62)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{3!}\mathbf{w}^{(3,1)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(3,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})

(C.63)

\displaystyle\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)+\delta(p_{3}-p)-\delta(p_{4}-p)\big)

(C.64)

\displaystyle\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}-p_{4})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{4})\big)}

(C.65)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})f_{0}(p_{4})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{4})\big)

(C.66)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4!}\mathbf{w}^{(4,0)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(4,0)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{4})

(C.67)

\displaystyle\big(\delta(p_{1}-p)+\delta(p_{2}-p)+\delta(p_{3}-p)+\delta(p_{4}-p)\big)

(C.68)

\displaystyle\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}+p_{4})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \big(\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{4})\big)}

(C.69)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{4})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})f_{0}(p_{4})\big)\Big)\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.2

(Calculation of collision operators for
(\Phi,g)
)
.

Recall from (
2.74
) and (
2.75
) that
\reflectbox{$\vec{\reflectbox{$\mathbf{p}$}}$}_{3}=(p_{3},p_{2},p_{1})
and
\overline{\mathbf{p}}=(p_{1},p_{2},-p_{3})
. Using the notation in Lemma
A.2
, the cubic Boltzmann operator for
\Phi
is given by

(C.70)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ Q_{3}^{(\Phi)}[f_{0}](s)\,=

(C.71)

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{s}_{2}\ \mathds{1}_{s_{1}\geq s_{2}}e^{i\int_{0}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(0)}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})\Big(e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}

(C.72)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\mathbf{w}^{(3,1)}_{s_{1}}(0,\mathbf{p}_{3})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\,-\,e^{-i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}\mathbf{w}^{(2,2)}_{s_{1}}(0,\reflectbox{$\vec{\reflectbox{$\mathbf{p}$}}$}_{3})\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{s_{2}}(\reflectbox{$\vec{\reflectbox{$\mathbf{p}$}}$}_{3})\Big)

(C.73)

\displaystyle\qquad\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)

(C.74)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\frac{1}{3!}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})\Big(\mathbf{w}^{(4,0)}_{s_{1}}(0,\mathbf{p}_{3})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(3,0)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}

(C.75)

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\,\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(3,1)}_{s_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{3},0)\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})e^{-i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}\Big)

(C.76)

\displaystyle\qquad\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\big)\Big]\,,

and for
g
, it is given by

(C.77)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ Q_{3}^{(g)}[f_{0}](s)[J]\,=

(C.78)

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\int\mathrm{d}p\ J(p)\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{s}_{2}\ \mathds{1}_{s_{1}\geq s_{2}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{3}\ \Big[\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})\Big(\delta(p-p_{3})e^{2i\int_{0}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})}

(C.79)

\displaystyle\quad e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{s_{1}}(\overline{\mathbf{p}}_{3})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})-2\delta(p-p_{1})e^{-2i\int_{0}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})}

(C.80)

\displaystyle\quad e^{-i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{1}}(\overline{\mathbf{p}}_{3})\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\Big)

(C.81)

\displaystyle\quad\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)

(C.82)

\displaystyle+\,\delta(p-p_{3})e^{2i\int_{0}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})e^{-i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}

(C.83)

\displaystyle\quad\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{1}}(\overline{\mathbf{p}}_{3})\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)\Big]\,.

Proof.

C.1
, we obtain

(C.85)

\displaystyle=\,

\displaystyle\qquad\big(f_{0}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)\,.

Q_{3}^{(\Phi)}[f_{0}](t)
, we substitute
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{3}
in the last term.

\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{{

{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}{}{{}}{}{

{}{}{
