{}{}{
{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}
{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 4.84 L 0 26.29 L 0 26.29 L 0 4.84}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\hskip-15.29172pt{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{cub}}\vbox to19.00002pt{}{}(s_{2})]\rangle_{0}

(C.93)

\displaystyle=\,

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\int\mathrm{d}p\ J(p)\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{s}_{2}\ \mathds{1}_{s_{1}\geq s_{2}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{3}\ \Big[\delta(p-p_{3})e^{2i\int_{0}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p_{3})}

(C.94)

\displaystyle\Big(\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})e^{i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{s_{1}}(\overline{\mathbf{p}}_{3})\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})

(C.95)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)

(C.96)

\displaystyle+\,\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})e^{-i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{1}}(\overline{\mathbf{p}}_{3})\mathbf{w}^{(3,0)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})

(C.97)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})\big)\Big)

(C.98)

\displaystyle+2\delta(p-p_{1})e^{-2i\int_{0}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})e^{-i\int_{s_{2}}^{s_{1}}\mathrm{d}\tau\ (\Omega_{\tau}(p_{1})+\Omega_{\tau}(p_{2})-\Omega_{\tau}(p_{3}))}

(C.99)

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{w}}^{(2,1)}_{s_{1}}(\overline{\mathbf{p}}_{3})\mathbf{w}^{(2,1)}_{s_{2}}(\mathbf{p}_{3})\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{3})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{3})\big)\,.

Rearranging the terms yields the result.
∎

Appendix D
Trace estimates

Lemma D.1

(Number operator moments)
.

We have for all
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
that

(D.1)

\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\rangle_{0}\,\leq\,C_{\ell,\|f_{0}\|_{X}}|\Lambda|^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\,.

Lemma D.2

(Operator product bound)
.

Let
A_{j}\in{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{\dagger}]
be monomials in
a,a^{\dagger}
,
\gamma_{j}>0
, and
k_{j}\in{\mathbb{N}}
be such that

(D.2)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}A_{j}P_{m-\osg(A_{j})}\|\,\leq\,\gamma_{j}(m+|\Lambda|)^{k_{j}/2}

for all
j\in\{1,\ldots,\ell\}
and all
m\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
. Then we have that

(D.3)

\displaystyle|\nu(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}A_{j})|\,\leq\,\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big)\nu\Big(\big({\mathcal{N}}+\sum_{m=1}^{\ell}|\osg(A_{m})|+|\Lambda|\big)^{\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}k_{j}/2}\Big)

for any state
\nu
.

Lemma D.3
.

Given a test function
J\in L^{2}\cap L^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})
, let

(D.4)

\displaystyle f[J]:=

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,J(p)a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\,,

(D.5)

\displaystyle g[J]:=

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,J(p)a_{-p}a_{p}\,.

Then we have

(D.6)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}f[J]P_{n}\|\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{m,n}\|J\|_{\infty}m\,,

(D.7)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}g[J]P_{n}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\delta_{n,m+2}(\|J\|_{2}+\|J\|_{\infty})(m+1+|\Lambda|)\,.

For the following standard result, we need to introduce some notation. For a proof of the statement, we refer, e.g., to
[
5
,
6
]
. Denote

(D.8)

\displaystyle a_{p}^{(1)}\,:=\,a^{\dagger}_{p}\,,\qquad a_{p}^{(-1)}\,:=\,a_{p}\,.

Given a finite ordered subset
J=\{j_{1}<j_{2}<\ldots<j_{r}\}\subset{\mathbb{N}}
and
\sigma_{j_{k}}\in\{\pm 1\}
, we define the ordered product

(D.9)

\displaystyle\prod_{j\in J}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}\,:=\,a_{p_{j_{1}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{1}})}\ldots a_{p_{j_{r}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{r}})}\,.

In addition, we abbreviate

(D.10)

\displaystyle\mathbf{p}_{J}\,:=\,(p_{j_{k}})_{k=1}^{r}\,,

as well as

(D.11)

\displaystyle a^{(\sigma)}(\mathbf{p}_{J})\,:=\,\prod_{j\in J}a^{(\sigma)}_{p_{j}}\,.

Note that we have that

(D.12)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},\prod_{j\in J}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}]\,=\,\sum_{j\in J}\sigma_{j}\prod_{j\in J}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}\,.

Furthermore, we define the sets

(D.13)

\displaystyle J_{\pm}\,:=\,\{j\in J\mid\sigma_{j}=\pm 1\}

and the Wick-ordered product

(D.14)

\displaystyle:\prod_{j\in J}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}:\,:=\,a^{\dagger}(\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}})a(\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}})

with all creation operators to the left, and all annihilation operators to the right.

Finally, in order to keep track of the correct scaling, it is useful to work with the rescaled
\ell^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})
-norm

(D.15)

\displaystyle\|H\|_{L^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})}\,=\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}\|H\|_{\ell^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})}\,.

More generally, we also define

(D.16)

\displaystyle\|H\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{m}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{k}_{n}}(({\Lambda^{*}})^{m+n})}\,:=\,\sup_{\mathbf{p}_{m}\in({\Lambda^{*}})^{m}}\Big(\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{k}_{n}|H(\mathbf{p}_{m},\mathbf{k}_{n})|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,,

(D.17)

\displaystyle\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{k}_{n}}L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{m}}(({\Lambda^{*}})^{m+n})}\,:=\,\Big(\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{k}_{n}\sup_{\mathbf{p}_{m}\in({\Lambda^{*}})^{m}}|H(\mathbf{p}_{m},\mathbf{k}_{n})|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,,

where in the case
n=0
, this norm reduces to
\|H\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{m}}(({\Lambda^{*}})^{m})}
, and in the case
m=0
, to
\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{k}_{n}}(({\Lambda^{*}})^{n})}
.

Lemma D.4

(Wick-ordered operator bound)
.

Let
M\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
,
J:=\{1,\ldots,n\}
,
\sigma_{j}\in\{\pm 1\}
for all
j\in J
. Let
H:({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}\to{\mathbb{C}}
, and
g_{j}:{\Lambda^{*}}\to{\mathbb{C}}
be given functions. Then the following holds true

(1)

If
J_{\pm}\neq\emptyset
, we have that

(D.18)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j}\sigma_{j})\prod_{j=1}^{n}g_{j}(p_{j}):\prod_{j=1}^{n}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}:P_{M}\Big\|

(D.19)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\||H|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j\in J_{-}}g_{j}(p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}}\||H|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j\in J_{+}}g_{j}(p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}}

(D.20)

\displaystyle(M+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j})_{|J_{+}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}(M)_{|J_{-}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathds{1}_{M\geq|J_{-}|}\,,

where

(D.21)

\displaystyle(x)_{m}\,:=\,\prod_{k=0}^{m-1}(x-k)

denotes the falling factorial.

(2)

If
J_{+}=\emptyset
and
n\geq 2
, we find that

(D.22)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a(\mathbf{p}_{n})P_{M}\Big\|

(D.23)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\big(\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n-2}L^{\infty}_{p_{n-1},p_{n}}}}^{2}(M-n+1)+|\Lambda|\|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n}}}^{2}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}(M)_{n-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Similarly, in the case
J_{-}=\emptyset
, we have that

(D.24)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\,H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a^{\dagger}(\mathbf{p}_{n})P_{M}\Big\|

(D.25)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n-2}}L^{\infty}_{p_{n-1},p_{n}}}^{2}(M+1)+|\Lambda|\|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n}}}^{2}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}(M+n)_{n-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

(3)

If
n=1
, we obtain

(D.26)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}P_{M}\|\,=\,\sqrt{M|\Lambda|}\,.

Appendix E
Bounds on fluctuation dynamics

Lemma E.1

(Bogoliubov dynamics)
.

Assume that
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. For any
t\geq 0
, let
u_{t}
,
v_{t}
be defined as in (
2.27
), such that
\gamma_{t}:=|v_{t}|^{2}\in L^{1}\cap L^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})
. Then, for any
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}
, there exist constants
C_{\ell}>0
such that for any
t>0
we have that

(E.1)

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}{\mathcal{T}}[k_{0}](\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\big\|\,

\displaystyle\leq\,C_{\ell}(1+\|\gamma_{0}\|_{1}+\|\gamma_{0}\|_{\infty})^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\,,

(E.2)

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}{\mathcal{T}}^{\dagger}[k_{t}](\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\big\|\,

\displaystyle\leq\,C_{\ell}(1+\|\gamma_{t}\|_{1}+\|\gamma_{t}\|_{\infty})^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\,.

Proof.

Recall the facts that
u_{t}(p)^{2}=1+|v_{t}(p)|^{2}=1+\gamma_{t}
, see (
2.30
), and that
\gamma_{t}
is even. Employing (
2.29
) and Lemma
D.3
, we obtain

(E.3)

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathcal{T}}^{\dagger}[k_{0}]\mathcal{N}_{b}{\mathcal{T}}[k_{0}]\,&=\,\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}\mathrm{d}p\ (u_{0}(p)a^{\dagger}_{p}+\overline{v}_{0}(p)a_{-p})(u_{0}(p)a_{p}+v_{0}(p)a^{\dagger}_{-p})\\
&=\,\mathcal{N}_{b}+\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}\mathrm{d}p\ \big(2\gamma_{0}(p)a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}+(\sigma_{0}(p)a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}+\mathrm{h.c.})+\,|\Lambda|\gamma_{0}(p)\big)\,.\end{split}

Lemma
D.3
implies

(E.4)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}f[1+2\gamma_{0}]P_{n}\|\,

\displaystyle\leq\,\delta_{m,n}(1+2\|\gamma_{0}\|_{\infty})m\,,

(E.5)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}g[\overline{\sigma}_{0}]P_{n}\|\,

\displaystyle\leq\,\delta_{n,m+2}(\|\sigma_{0}\|_{2}+\|\sigma_{0}\|_{\infty})(m+1+|\Lambda|)\,.

Due to
|\sigma|^{2}=\gamma(\gamma+1)
, see (
2.32
), we have

(E.6)

\displaystyle\|\sigma\|_{2}\,

\displaystyle\leq\,\|\gamma\|_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+\|\gamma\|_{\infty})^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\leq\,\frac{1}{2}(1+\|\gamma\|_{1}+\|\gamma\|_{\infty})\,,

(E.7)

\displaystyle\|\sigma\|_{\infty}\,

\displaystyle\leq\,\|\gamma\|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+\|\gamma\|_{\infty})^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\leq\,\frac{1}{2}(1+2\|\gamma\|_{\infty})\,.

As a consequence, (
E.5
) implies

(E.8)

\|P_{m}g[\overline{\sigma}_{0}]P_{n}\|\,\leq\,\frac{\delta_{n,m+2}}{2}(2+\|\gamma_{0}\|_{1}+3\|\gamma_{0}\|_{\infty})(m+1+|\Lambda|)\,.

Using
|\Lambda|\geq 1
, Lemma
D.2
then yields

(E.9)

\displaystyle\langle\psi,{\mathcal{T}}^{\dagger}[k_{0}](\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}{\mathcal{T}}[k_{0}]\psi\rangle\,

\displaystyle=\,\langle\psi,\big({\mathcal{T}}^{\dagger}[k_{0}]\mathcal{N}_{b}{\mathcal{T}}[k_{0}]+|\Lambda|\big)^{\ell}\psi\rangle

(E.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\ell}\,(1+\|\gamma_{0}\|_{1}+\|\gamma_{0}\|_{\infty})^{\ell}\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+\ell\big)^{\ell}\psi\rangle

(E.11)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\ell}\,(1+\|\gamma_{0}\|_{1}+\|\gamma_{0}\|_{\infty})^{\ell}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}\psi\rangle\,.

With analogous steps, we obtain

(E.12)

\displaystyle\langle\psi,{\mathcal{T}}[k_{t}](\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}{\mathcal{T}}^{\dagger}[k_{t}]\psi\rangle\,\lesssim_{\ell}\,(1+\|\gamma_{t}\|_{1}+\|\gamma_{t}\|_{\infty})^{\ell}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}\psi\rangle\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma E.2

(BEC fluctuation dynamics)
.

Assume
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. Let
\phi\in L^{\infty}(0,t)
for all
t>0
. Define
\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t):={\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]e^{-it{\mathcal{H}}_{N}}{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]
. Then there are
C_{\ell},K_{\ell}>0
such that

(E.13)

\displaystyle\Big\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|

(E.14)

\displaystyle\leq\,C_{\ell}\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{\ell}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}

for all
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}
.

Proof.

We need to reprove this statement as, compared to
[
37
]
, here,
\hat{v}(0)\neq 0
and
\phi
is not stationary. Nonetheless, we follow the steps of the proof in
[
29
]
. We show the statement by induction on
\ell
. Let
\psi\in{\mathcal{F}}
be arbitrary with
\|\psi\|=1
.

(1)

Induction basis:
Define

(E.15)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}^{(\phi_{t})}\,

\displaystyle:=\,\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}a^{\dagger}_{0}(-i\partial_{t}\phi_{t}+\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)|\phi_{t}|^{2}\phi_{t})\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

(E.16)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{(\mathrm{cor},\phi_{t})}\,

\displaystyle:=\,\frac{\lambda|\Lambda|}{2}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \hat{v}(p)\big(\phi_{t}^{2}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}+\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}a_{p}a_{-p}\big)

(E.17)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{cub}}^{(\phi_{t})}\,

\displaystyle:=\,\frac{\lambda\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{2}\ \hat{v}(p_{2})\big(a^{\dagger}_{p_{1}}a^{\dagger}_{p_{2}}a_{p_{12}}\phi_{t}\,+\,a^{\dagger}_{p_{12}}a_{p_{2}}a_{p_{1}}\overline{\phi}_{t}\big).

Substituting (
4.2
) and recalling definitions (
2.57
), we find that

(E.18)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}^{(\phi_{t})}\,=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{\lambda\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}a^{\dagger}_{0}\Big(\big(\Gamma^{T}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\phi_{t}+(\Sigma^{T}_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\phi_{t}\Big)+\mathrm{h.c.}

Then (
A.66
) and (
A.73
) imply that

(E.19)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&i\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&=\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}^{(\phi_{t})}+{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{(\mathrm{cor},\phi_{t})}+{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{cub}}^{(\phi_{t})}]\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Recall from (
D.12
) that for any monomial
A\in{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{\dagger}]
we have

(E.20)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},A]=

\displaystyle\osg(A)A\,.

Employing Lemmata
D.3
and
D.4
together with Young’s inequality, (
E.19
) yields

(E.21)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

(E.22)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C(\|\hat{v}\|_{1}+\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty})\lambda\Big(\frac{|\Lambda|}{\sqrt{N}}(\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{1}+\|\Sigma^{T}_{t}\|_{2}+\|\Sigma^{T}_{t}\|_{\infty})|\phi_{t}|

(E.23)

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.24)

\displaystyle+\,|\phi_{t}|^{2}|\Lambda|\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.25)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{|\phi_{t}|\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{3}{2}}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)

(E.26)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C(\|\hat{v}\|_{1}+\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty})\lambda|\Lambda|\Big(\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{\infty})^{2}}{N}

(E.27)

\displaystyle+\,|\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.28)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)\,,

where in the last step, we used Cauchy-Schwarz together with Lemma
4.5
.
Our goal now is to bound
\frac{1}{N}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle
in terms of
\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle
and time-dependent
\psi
-independent terms, in order to close the estimate. Using

(E.29)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]a_{p}{\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]\,=\,a_{p}\,-\,\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}\delta(p)\,,

we derive that

(E.30)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]]\,=\,

\displaystyle-\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}{\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}](\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})

(E.31)

\displaystyle+N|\Lambda|{\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]

(E.32)

\displaystyle=\,

\displaystyle-\big(\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})+N|\Lambda|\big){\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]\,,

(E.33)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]]=\,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\big(\sqrt{N}(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})+N|\Lambda|\big)\,.

From these identities and using
[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{H}}_{N}]=0
and
\phi_{0}=|\Lambda|^{-\frac{1}{2}}
, we obtain that

(E.34)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)]=

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]]e^{-it{\mathcal{H}}_{N}}{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]

(E.35)

\displaystyle+\,{\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]e^{-it{\mathcal{H}}_{N}}[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]]

(E.36)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\,+\,\sqrt{N}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\,.

As a consequence, we have that

(E.37)

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.38)

\displaystyle=\,

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{N}_{b}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\mathcal{N}_{b}\psi\rangle\,+\,\sqrt{N}\langle\mathcal{N}_{b}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\,

(E.39)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\langle\mathcal{N}_{b}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

Using Cauchy-Schwarz and employing the fact that
\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t):{\mathcal{F}}\to{\mathcal{F}}
is a unitary transformation, we thus obtain

(E.40)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
\leq\,&\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\Big(\|\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\mathcal{N}_{b}\psi\|+\sqrt{N}\big(\sqrt{|\Lambda|}\|(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\\
&+\,\|\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\|\big)\Big)\\
\leq\,&\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\Big(\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\psi\|+\sqrt{N}\big(\sqrt{|\Lambda|}|\phi_{t}|(\|a_{0}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\\
&+\,\|a^{\dagger}_{0}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|)+\,\|a_{0}\psi\|\,+\,\|a^{\dagger}_{0}\psi\|\big)\Big)\,.\end{split}

Lemma
D.4
implies

(E.41)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|a_{0}\psi\|^{2}\,=&\,\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\|P_{M}a_{0}\psi\|^{2}\,=\,\sum_{M=1}^{\infty}\|a_{0}P_{M-1}\psi\|^{2}\\
\leq&\,\sum_{M=1}^{\infty}(M-1)|\Lambda|\|P_{M-1}\psi\|^{2}\,=\,|\Lambda|\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}}\psi\|^{2}\,.\end{split}

Similarly, we have that

(E.42)

\displaystyle\|a^{\dagger}_{0}\psi\|\,\leq\,\sqrt{|\Lambda|}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}+1}\psi\|

Employing (
E.41
) and (
E.42
), (
E.40
) implies

(E.43)

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.44)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\Big(\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\psi\|+2\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\big(\sqrt{|\Lambda|}|\phi_{t}|\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|

(E.45)

\displaystyle\,+\,\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}+1}\psi\|\big)\Big)\,.

Young’s inequality then implies

(E.46)

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.47)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle+C\Big(\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}+N|\Lambda|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)\big)\psi\rangle

(E.48)

\displaystyle\,+\,N|\Lambda|^{2}|\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)

Rearranging terms, we thus obtain that

(E.49)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
\leq&\,C\Big(|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle+\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\Big)\,.\end{split}

Substituting this into (
E.28
) and using
|\Lambda|\geq 1
, we obtain that

(E.50)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

(E.51)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|\Big(\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{\infty})^{2}}{N}

(E.52)

\displaystyle+|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle+\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\Big)\,.

Lemma
4.5
then implies

(E.53)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

(E.54)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|\Big(e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})\lambda t}\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}

(E.55)

\displaystyle+\Big(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N}\Big)\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle+\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\Big)

Grönwall’s inequality then implies

(E.56)

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.57)

\displaystyle\leq\,C_{1}\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)e^{K_{1}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})\lambda|\Lambda|t}

(E.58)

\displaystyle\qquad\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,.

(2)

Induction Step:
Assume that

(E.59)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
\leq\,&C_{j}\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}\\
&\,\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\end{split}

for all
1\leq j\leq\ell
and some constants
C_{j},K_{j}
, and any
\psi\in{\mathcal{F}}
. We compute

(E.60)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&i\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
=&\,\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\big[(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1},{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}^{(\phi_{t})}+{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{(\mathrm{cor},\phi_{t})}+{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{cub}}^{(\phi_{t})}]\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
=&\,\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\big\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}\big[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}^{(\phi_{t})}+{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{(\mathrm{cor},\phi_{t})}+{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{cub}}^{(\phi_{t})}\big]\\
&\quad(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\big\rangle\,.\end{split}

Let

(E.61)

\displaystyle A_{\mathrm{cub}}[\hat{v}]\,:=\,\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{p}_{3}\ \hat{v}(p_{2})\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})a^{\dagger}_{p_{1}}a^{\dagger}_{p_{2}}a_{p_{3}}\,.

Using (
D.12
) and recalling Lemma
D.3
, (
E.60
) yields

(E.62)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&i\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
=&\,2\lambda\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\operatorname{Im}\Big(-\frac{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\big(\big(\Gamma^{T}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\phi_{t}+(\Sigma^{T}_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)\\
&\qquad\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}a^{\dagger}_{0}(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&\qquad+|\Lambda|\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}g[\hat{v}](\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&\qquad-\frac{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}}{\sqrt{N}}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}A_{\mathrm{cub}}[\hat{v}](\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
=&\,2\lambda\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\operatorname{Im}\sum_{m,n=0}^{\infty}(m+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\\
&\,\Big\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,P_{m}\Big(-\frac{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\big(\big(\Gamma^{T}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\phi_{t}\\
&\,+(\Sigma^{T}_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}\big)a^{\dagger}_{0}+|\Lambda|\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}g[\hat{v}]-\frac{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}}{\sqrt{N}}A_{\mathrm{cub}}[\hat{v}]\Big)P_{n}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\Big\rangle\,.\end{split}

Observe that we have

(E.63)

\displaystyle P_{m}g[\hat{v}]P_{n}=

\displaystyle P_{m}g[\hat{v}]P_{m+2}\delta_{n,m+2}\,,

(E.64)

\displaystyle P_{m}A_{cub}[\hat{v}]P_{n}=

\displaystyle P_{m}A_{cub}[\hat{v}]P_{m-1}\delta_{n,m-1}\,.

Lemmata
D.3
and
D.4
, and (
E.76
) then imply

(E.65)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

(E.66)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\lambda\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\sum_{m,n=0}^{\infty}(m+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\|P_{m}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\|P_{n}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|

(E.67)

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}(\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{\infty})|\phi_{t}|\|a^{\dagger}_{0}P_{m-1}\|\delta_{n,m-1}

(E.68)

\displaystyle\,+|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\|g[\hat{v}]P_{m+2}\|\delta_{n,m+2}\,+\,\frac{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}|\phi_{t}|\|A_{cub}[\hat{v}]P_{m-1}\|}{\sqrt{N}}\delta_{n,m-1}\Big)

(E.69)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\sum_{m,n=0}^{\infty}(m+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}

(E.70)

\displaystyle\big(\|P_{m}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|^{2}+\|P_{n}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|^{2}\big)

(E.71)

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{|\Lambda|}{\sqrt{N}}(\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{\infty})|\phi_{t}|m^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta_{n,m-1}

(E.72)

\displaystyle\,+\,|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}(m+2+|\Lambda|)\delta_{n,m+2}\,+\,\frac{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}|\phi_{t}|m^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{N}}\delta_{n,m-1}\Big)

(E.73)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}(m+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(m+2+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\|P_{m}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|^{2}

(E.74)

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{\infty})^{2}}{N}+|\phi_{t}|^{2}(m+2+|\Lambda|)+\frac{(m+1)^{2}}{N|\Lambda|}\Big)\,,

where, in the last step, we applied Cauchy-Schwarz. We can rewrite the last inequality as

(E.75)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|\\
\leq\,&K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|\Big(\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{t}\|_{\infty})^{2}}{N}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,|\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)\,.\end{split}

The induction hypothesis (
E.59
) and Lemma
4.5
then imply

(E.76)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|\\
\leq\,&C_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|\Big[\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{\ell}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}\\
&\,\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\Big(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N}\Big)\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\frac{1}{N}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big]\,.\end{split}

We claim that

(E.77)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
\leq\,&C_{j}\Big(\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}\\
&\,\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\Big(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N}\Big)\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)\end{split}

for all
1\leq j\leq\ell+1
and all
\psi\in{\mathcal{F}}
. (
E.77
) for
j=\ell+1
together with (
E.76
) then implies

(E.78)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

(E.79)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\Big(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N}\Big)\lambda|\Lambda|

(E.80)

\displaystyle\Big(\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle+e^{K_{\ell+1}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}

(E.81)

\displaystyle\,\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{\ell+1}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,.

Grönwall’s Lemma then implies

(E.82)

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

(E.83)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C_{\ell+1}e^{K_{\ell+1}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{\ell+1}

(E.84)

\displaystyle\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,.

Thus, proving (
E.77
) for
j=\ell+1
concludes the proof. We have proved (
E.77
) for
j=1
in Step 1, (
E.49
). We have that (
E.77
) also holds for
j=0
: In fact, for

(E.85)

\displaystyle\langle\Psi,(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\Psi\rangle\,

\displaystyle\leq\,|\phi_{t}|\|\Psi\|(\|a_{0}\Psi\|+\|a^{\dagger}_{0}\Psi\|)

(E.86)

\displaystyle\leq\,2|\phi_{t}|\sqrt{|\Lambda|}\|\Psi\|\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}+1}\Psi\|

(E.87)

\displaystyle\leq\,\langle\Psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1+|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2})\Psi\rangle\,,

due to (
E.41
) and (
E.42
) followed by Young’s inequality, we find that

(E.88)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]\mathcal{N}_{b}{\mathcal{W}}^{\dagger}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{t}]

(E.89)

\displaystyle=\,

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{b}-\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})+N|\Lambda|^{2}|\phi_{t}|^{2}

(E.90)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle 2(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+N|\Lambda|^{2}|\phi_{t}|^{2})\,,

which then commutes with
e^{-it{\mathcal{H}}_{N}}
.

Suppose (
E.77
) holds up to some
1\leq j\leq\ell
. Applying (
E.36
), we have that

(E.91)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
=&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\\
&-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Whenever
\phi_{t}\neq 0
, we can bound the second term by

(E.92)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
\leq&\alpha|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\frac{\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle}{\alpha N|\Lambda|^{2}|\phi_{t}|^{2}}\,.\end{split}

Employing (
E.41
) and (
E.42
), we find that, for any
\tilde{\psi}\in{\mathcal{F}}
and
k\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,

(E.93)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\langle\tilde{\psi},(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k}(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\tilde{\psi}\rangle\\
\leq\,&|\phi_{t}|^{2}(\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{k}{2}}a_{0}\tilde{\psi}\|+\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{k}{2}}a^{\dagger}_{0}\tilde{\psi}\|)^{2}\\
=\,&|\phi_{t}|^{2}(\|a_{0}(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|-1)^{\frac{k}{2}}\tilde{\psi}\|+\|a^{\dagger}_{0}(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+1)^{\frac{k}{2}}\tilde{\psi}\|)^{2}\\
\leq\,&C_{k}|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\tilde{\psi},(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k+1}\tilde{\psi}\rangle\,,\end{split}

i.e.,

(E.94)

\displaystyle(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k}(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\,\leq\,C_{k}|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k+1}\,.

Analogously, we have that

(E.95)

(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k}(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\,\leq\,C_{k}|\Lambda|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k+1}\,.

Employing (
E.94
) and choosing
\alpha>0
sufficiently large, (
E.92
) implies

(E.96)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
\leq&C_{j}|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\frac{1}{4N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

We bound the third term in (
E.91
) by

(E.97)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\big|

(E.98)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle

(E.99)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

In particular, (
E.91
), (
E.96
), and (
E.97
) imply

(E.100)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
\leq&\,\frac{2}{N|\Lambda|}|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle|\\
&\,+\,C_{j}|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&\,+\,\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

For the first term in (
E.100
), we apply (
E.36
) to the left and obtain

(E.101)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\\
=\,&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\\
&-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

With analogous steps to above, we bound the second term in (
E.101
) by

(E.102)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}(\overline{\phi}_{t}a_{0}+\phi_{t}a^{\dagger}_{0})\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\big|\\
\leq\,&C_{j}|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\,,\end{split}

and the third term by

(E.103)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\big|\\
\leq\,&\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

In particular, we can bound (
E.101
) by

(E.104)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle|

(E.105)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{3}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

(E.106)

\displaystyle+\,C_{j}|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.107)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\,.

As a consequence, (
E.100
) implies

(E.108)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
\leq&\,\frac{6}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\\
&\,+\,C_{j}|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\\
&\,+\,\frac{2}{|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

For the first term in (
E.101
), we use the induction hypothesis (
E.77
), and obtain

(E.109)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\\
\leq\,&C_{j-1}\Big(\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{j-1}e^{K_{j-1}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}\\
&\,\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\\
&+\,\Big(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N}\Big)\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\Big)\\
\leq\,&C_{j}\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}\\
&\,\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\end{split}

where in the last step, we employed the induction hypothesis (
E.59
).

For the third term in (
E.101
), we use the induction hypothesis (
E.59
) to obtain

(E.110)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{2}{|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\\
&\leq\,\frac{C_{j}}{|\Lambda|}\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}\\
&\,\langle(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)(a_{0}+a^{\dagger}_{0})\psi\rangle\\
&\leq\,C_{j}\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}\\
&\,\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,,\end{split}

where, in the last step, we employed (
E.95
).

Substituting (
E.109
) and (
E.110
) into (
E.108
), we arrive at

(E.111)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

(E.112)

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle C_{j}\Big(1+\frac{(\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}+\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{\infty}+1)^{2}}{N}\Big)^{j+1}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{Y}\lambda|\Lambda|(1+\frac{\|\Gamma^{T}_{0}\|_{1}}{N})t}

(E.113)

\displaystyle\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

(E.114)

\displaystyle+\,C_{j}|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\langle\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widehat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

This concludes the proof.

∎

Lemma E.3

(Expressions for
{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}
and
{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}
)
.

Assume that
(\phi^{(2)},\gamma^{(2)},\sigma^{(2)})
satisfy (
3.77
) and that
\Omega^{(2)}
satisfies (
3.81
). Then we have

(E.115)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}(t)\,=

(E.116)

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\Big[u_{t}(0)\Big(2(f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}+\big((1+2\gamma_{t})f_{0}^{(+)}\big)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))(0)\phi_{t}\Big)

(E.117)

\displaystyle+\,v_{t}(0)\Big(2(f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\phi_{t}+\big((1+2\gamma_{t})f_{0}^{(+)}\big)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}\Big)e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \Omega_{s}(0)}a^{\dagger}_{0}

(E.118)

\displaystyle+\,\mathrm{h.c.}\,,

(E.119)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(d)}}(t)\,=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[2\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big((f_{0}^{(+)}\overline{\sigma}^{(2)}_{t})*\hat{v}\big)(p)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)\Big)

(E.120)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\big((f_{0}^{(+)}(1+2\gamma^{(2)}))*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)\big(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)\big)\Big]a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\,,

(E.121)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(cor)}}(t)\,=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[\Big(\big(f_{0}^{(+)}(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t})\big)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\Big)(p)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)

(E.122)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\big((f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma^{(2)}_{t})*\hat{v}\big)(p)(1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p))

(E.123)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\big((f_{0}^{(+)}\overline{\sigma}^{(2)}_{t})*\hat{v}\big)(p)\frac{\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)^{2}}{1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)}\Big]e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega^{(2)}_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

Proof.

We start by computing
{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}(t)
. By Lemma
A.2
, we have that

(E.124)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}(t)\,=\,

(E.125)

\displaystyle\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\Big[u_{t}(0)\Big(-i\partial_{t}\phi_{t}+\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)|\phi_{t}|^{2}\phi_{t}+\frac{\lambda}{N}(\sigma_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}

(E.126)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\gamma_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\phi_{t}\Big)\,+\,v_{t}(0)\Big(-\overline{i\partial_{t}\phi_{t}}+\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)|\phi_{t}|^{2}\overline{\phi}_{t}

(E.127)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{N}(\overline{\sigma}_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\phi_{t}+\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\gamma_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}\Big)\Big]e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \Omega_{s}(0)}a^{\dagger}_{0}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

Recalling (
3.77
),
\phi^{(2)}
satisfies

(E.128)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\phi^{(2)}_{t}\,=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\Big(\big(\Gamma^{(2)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(0)\phi^{(2)}_{t}\,+\,(\Sigma^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}^{(2)}_{t}\Big)

(E.129)

\displaystyle-\,2\lambda|\Lambda|\hat{v}(0)|\phi^{(2)}_{t}|^{2}\phi^{(2)}_{t}\,.

With that, (
E.124
) implies

(E.130)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{BEC}}(t)\,=

(E.131)

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}{\sqrt{N}}\Big[u_{t}(0)\Big(2(f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}+\big((1+2\gamma_{t})f_{0}^{(+)}\big)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))(0)\phi_{t}\Big)

(E.132)

\displaystyle+\,v_{t}(0)\Big(2(f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma_{t}*\hat{v})(0)\phi_{t}+\big((1+2\gamma_{t})f_{0}^{(+)}\big)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))(0)\overline{\phi}_{t}\Big)e^{i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\ \Omega_{s}(0)}a^{\dagger}_{0}

(E.133)

\displaystyle+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

Similarly, Lemma
A.2
implies

(E.134)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(d)}}(t)\,=\,

(E.135)

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[-\Omega_{t}(p)-\frac{\operatorname{Re}\big(\overline{\sigma}_{t}(p)i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}(p)\big)}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}

(E.136)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\big((\gamma_{t}+N|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\delta)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)\Big)\big(1+2\gamma_{t}(p)\big)

(E.137)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big((\overline{\sigma}_{t}+N|\Lambda|\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}\delta)*\hat{v}\big)(p)\sigma_{t}(p)\Big)\Big]a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}

By (
3.77
), we have that

(E.138)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\sigma^{(2)}_{t}\,=\,2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(2)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t})\,,

while (
3.81
) implies

(E.139)

\displaystyle\Omega^{(2)}_{t}\,=\,E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Gamma^{(2)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\frac{\operatorname{Re}\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(2)}*\hat{v})\sigma^{(2)}_{t}\big)}{1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}}\,.

Using the fact that
|\sigma|^{2}=\gamma(\gamma+1)
, (
E.134
) thus implies

(E.140)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(d)}}(t)\,=\,

(E.141)

\displaystyle\quad\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[-\frac{\lambda}{N}\frac{\operatorname{Re}\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(2)}*\hat{v})(p)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)\big)}{1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)}\,+\,\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big((\overline{\sigma}^{(2)}_{t}+N|\Lambda|(\overline{\phi}^{(2)}_{t})^{2}\delta)*\hat{v}\big)(p)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)\Big)

(E.142)

\displaystyle\qquad-\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big(\overline{\Sigma}^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(p)\sigma^{(2)}(p)\Big)\frac{1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)}{1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)}\,-\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Gamma^{(2)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p))

(E.143)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big((\gamma^{(2)}_{t}+N|\Lambda||\phi^{(2)}_{t}|^{2}\delta)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)\big(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)\big)\Big]a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\,.

Simplifying the terms, recalling definitions (
3.56
) and (
3.57
), we obtain

(E.144)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(d)}}(t)\,=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[2\operatorname{Re}\Big(\big((f_{0}^{(+)}\overline{\sigma}^{(2)}_{t})*\hat{v}\big)(p)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)\Big)

(E.145)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\big((f_{0}^{(+)}(1+2\gamma^{(2)}))*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)\big(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)\big)\Big]a^{\dagger}_{p}a_{p}\,.

Finally, Lemma
A.2
yields

(E.146)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(cor)}}(t)\,=

\displaystyle\,\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[-\frac{i\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}(p)}{2}+\frac{\sigma_{t}(p)i\partial_{t}\gamma_{t}(p)}{2(1+\gamma_{t}(p))}

(E.147)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\big((\gamma_{t}+N|\Lambda||\phi_{t}|^{2}\delta)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)\Big)\sigma_{t}(p)

(E.148)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda}{2N}\Big(\big((\sigma_{t}+N|\Lambda|\phi_{t}^{2}\delta)*\hat{v}\big)(p)(1+\gamma_{t}(p))

(E.149)

\displaystyle+\,\big((\overline{\sigma}_{t}+N|\Lambda|\overline{\phi}_{t}^{2}\delta)*\hat{v}\big)(p)\frac{\sigma_{t}(p)^{2}}{1+\gamma_{t}(p)}\Big)\Big]e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

By (
3.77
), we have that

(E.150)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{t}\gamma^{(2)}_{t}\,=&\,\frac{2\lambda}{N}\operatorname{Im}\big((\Sigma^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v})\overline{\sigma}^{(2)}_{t}\big)\,=\,\frac{\lambda}{iN}\big((\Sigma^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v})\overline{\sigma}^{(2)}_{t}-(\overline{\Sigma}^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v})\sigma^{(2)}_{t}\big)\,,\\
i\partial_{t}\sigma^{(2)}_{t}\,=&\,2\big(E+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(2)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}\,+\,\frac{\lambda}{N}\big(\Sigma^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t})\,.\end{split}

Substituting these into (
E.146
), yields

(E.151)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(cor)}}(t)\,=\,

(E.152)

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[-\Big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\Gamma^{(2)}_{t}*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))(p)\Big)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)

(E.153)

\displaystyle-\,\frac{\lambda}{2N}\big(\Sigma^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v}\big)(p)(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p))

(E.154)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda}{2N}\Big((\Sigma^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v})(p)\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)-(\overline{\Sigma}^{(2)}_{t}*\hat{v})(p)\frac{\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)^{2}}{1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)}\Big)

(E.155)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(E(p)+\frac{\lambda}{N}\big((\gamma^{(2)}_{t}+N|\Lambda||\phi^{(2)}_{t}|^{2}\delta)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\big)(p)\Big)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)

(E.156)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda}{2N}\Big(\big((\sigma^{(2)}_{t}+N|\Lambda|(\phi^{(2)}_{t})^{2}\delta)*\hat{v}\big)(p)(1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p))

(E.157)

\displaystyle+\,\big((\overline{\sigma}^{(2)}_{t}+N|\Lambda|(\overline{\phi}^{(2)}_{t})^{2}\delta)*\hat{v}\big)(p)\frac{\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)^{2}}{1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)}\Big)\Big]e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega^{(2)}_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

We simplify this expression as

(E.158)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{HFB}}^{\mathrm{(cor)}}(t)\,=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\mathrm{d}p\ \Big[\Big(\big(f_{0}^{(+)}(1+2\gamma^{(2)}_{t})\big)*(\hat{v}+\hat{v}(0))\Big)(p)\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)

(E.159)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\big((f_{0}^{(+)}\sigma^{(2)}_{t})*\hat{v}\big)(p)(1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p))

(E.160)

\displaystyle\quad+\,\big((f_{0}^{(+)}\overline{\sigma}^{(2)}_{t})*\hat{v}\big)(p)\frac{\sigma^{(2)}_{t}(p)^{2}}{1+\gamma^{(2)}_{t}(p)}\Big]e^{2i\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\ \Omega^{(2)}_{\tau}(p)}a^{\dagger}_{p}a^{\dagger}_{-p}\,+\,\mathrm{h.c.}

∎
